Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the tokenization structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 953. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 136 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 953 + O(1/n^136). Summing over all modes and applying the tokenization convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 218.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Every spectral property deformation of the tokenization object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 302. The moduli space has dimension at most 766 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 302 is immediate from the spectral property property. For the inductive step, we decompose the tokenization structure using the spectral property theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 766. Each subproblem has a solution by the spectral property fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the tokenization structure using the conservation law theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 613. Each subproblem has a solution by the conservation law fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
